//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#8 — validate before any writable ownership or command lock.
pub(crate) fn existing_deployment(requested: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; run `snapback4 deploy {}` first", requested.display(), requested.display());
    if !requested.join("store.sqlite").is_file() { return Err(missing()); }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    inspect_current(&dir)?;
    Ok(dir)
}

// SQLite READONLY can create WAL sidecars. A stopped store's existing owner
// lock makes immutable inspection safe; a live host supplies its own status.
fn inspect_current(dir: &Path) -> Result<u64, String> {
    use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags, OptionalExtension};
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; run `snapback4 deploy {}` first", dir.display(), dir.display());
    let owner = match snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(&database,
        &snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&database), false, 0) {
        Ok(owner) => owner,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            // Relocated/legacy deployments may not yet have a sidecar beside
            // the resolved DB. Establish a complete current source first; only
            // a known deployment may let SQLite create its ordinary WAL files.
            return pointer_current(dir, &database);
        },
        Err(error) => return Err(format!("{}; existing ownership sidecar unavailable: {error}", missing())),
    };
    match owner.try_lock() {
        Ok(()) => {},
        Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => {
            match crate::release_control::request(dir, crate::release_control::Command::Status {after:0, items_after:None, event:None}) {
                Ok(status) => return status["release"].as_u64().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing),
                Err(error) => {
                    // A killed host's watchdogs retain ownership during their
                    // bounded cleanup. Preserve serve's existing restart wait.
                    if !crate::store_owner::stopped_host(&database) {
                        // A second source directory may alias the same DB. Its
                        // source cannot control this host, but its token still
                        // belongs to the resolved database. Establish current
                        // source before permitting ordinary read-only WAL work.
                        return pointer_current(dir, &database).map_err(|_| format!("{}; {error}",missing()));
                    }
                    let deadline=std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_secs(5);
                    loop {
                        match owner.try_lock() {
                            Ok(()) => break,
                            Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now()<deadline => std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(25)),
                            _ => return Err(format!("{}; {error}",missing())),
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        Err(error) => return Err(error.to_string()),
    }
    if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&database, &owner) {
        return Err("store ownership changed during release inspection; retry".into());
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    let bytes = { use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt; database.as_os_str().as_bytes() };
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    let bytes = database.to_str().ok_or("invalid store path")?.as_bytes();
    let mut uri = String::from("file:");
    for byte in bytes {
        if byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"/._-".contains(byte) { uri.push(*byte as char); }
        else { uri.push_str(&format!("%{byte:02X}")); }
    }
    let mut wal = database.as_os_str().to_os_string(); wal.push("-wal"); let wal = PathBuf::from(wal);
    let mut shm = database.as_os_str().to_os_string(); shm.push("-shm"); let shm = PathBuf::from(shm);
    let has_wal = match std::fs::metadata(&wal) {
        Ok(meta) => meta.len() > 0,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => false,
        Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
    };
    if has_wal && !shm.is_file() { return Err("store has a WAL without its shared-memory sidecar; run deploy on this stopped deployment to recover it before inspection".into()); }
    // Immutable SQLite omits WAL reads: use it only for the checkpointed main
    // image. Existing WAL+SHM can be read normally under the same exclusion.
    uri.push_str(if has_wal { "?mode=ro" } else { "?mode=ro&immutable=1" });
    let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(uri, OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_URI).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let release: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |row|row.get(0)).optional().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let release = match release {
        Some(value) => value,
        None => std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")).map_err(|_| missing())?,
    };
    release.trim().parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing)
}

fn pointer_current(dir: &Path, database: &Path) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; deploy first", dir.display());
    let pointer = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")).map_err(|_| missing())?;
    let pointer = pointer.trim().parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing)?;
    bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(pointer.to_string()))?;
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(database).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    match store.meta("release").map_err(|e|e.to_string())? {
        Some(value) => value.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing),
        None => Ok(pointer),
    }
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
        if target_release.is_some() {
            for warning in snapback4_lang::client_contracts::compare(&old,bound,&format!("release {n}")) {eprintln!("WARNING {warning}");}
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Status {
                after,
                items_after,
                event,
            },
        ),
    }
}
fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
    mut candidate: Option<&mut crate::release_files::Candidate>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request_observed(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
            |pending| { if let Some(candidate)=candidate.as_deref_mut() {candidate.pending=pending;} },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            let mut saved_version = super::saved_version::SavedVersion::prepare(&project,&sources,options,&dir)?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version { saved.finish(previous,&source_hash)?; }
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — equal retained executable bytes select their existing identity.
            let staged_digest = crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?;
            let mut matching = std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
                .filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e| e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok())
                .filter(|n| selectable(&dir, *n).is_ok())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
            matching.sort_unstable_by(|a,b| b.cmp(a));
            let mut matched = None;
            for n in matching {
                if crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(n.to_string()))? == staged_digest { matched=Some(n); break; }
            }
            if let Some(n) = matched {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();
                preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                preview_options.flags.insert("restore-dormant".into(),String::new());
                if !checking { preview_options.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new()); }
                super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),releases.join(n.to_string()),n,&preview_options)?;
                if checking { return Ok(()); }
                let selected = bound_at(releases.join(n.to_string()))?;
                activate(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),n,previous,revision,selected,false,true,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),options,None)?;
                if let Some(saved)=&mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash)?;}
                return Ok(());
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let mut validation=options.clone();
            validation.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
            validation.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new());
            super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&validation)?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema { return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into()); }
            n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
            let final_dir=releases.join(n.to_string());
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut candidate=crate::release_files::Candidate::new(&dir,n);
            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let result=activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                n,
                previous,
                revision,
                staged_bound,
                false,
                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                Some(&mut candidate),
            );
            match result {
                Ok(()) => {
                    candidate.committed=true;
                    if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");}
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash).map_err(|e|format!("release {n} committed, but saving its snapshot failed: {e}; retry unchanged deploy with --save-version"))?;}
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
                        loop {
                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
                        }
                        println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"code":"E_ADOPT","message":error,"dormant":inventory}));
                    }
                    return Err(error);
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "rollback",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let _deployment = target(&dir)?;
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let held = status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            }
            .ok_or("no current release")?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let selected = options
                .flags
                .get("to")
                .map(|n| {
                    if n=="latest" {return latest(&dir).map_err(|_| "no complete latest release");}
                    n.parse::<u64>()
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|n| *n > 0)
                        .ok_or("--to requires a positive immutable release id")
                })
                .transpose()?;
            let previous = selected
                .or_else(|| held["previous_active"].as_u64())
                .ok_or("nothing to roll back to")?;
            selectable(&dir, previous)?;
            let bound = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()))?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#14-wave-14-rollback-narration — resolve bare/latest selection visibly before any preview or live activation.
            let target_line=format!("resolved rollback target: release {previous} (current release {active})");
            if options.json {eprintln!("{target_line}");} else {println!("{target_line}");}
            check_change(&dir, Some(active), &bound, options, Some(previous))?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                return super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()),previous,options);
            }
            activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                previous,
                Some(active),
                revision,
                bound,
                true,
                true,
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                None,
            )?;

            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn releases(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "releases",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let after = options
                .flags
                .get("after")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--after requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?
                .unwrap_or(0);
            let event = options
                .flags
                .get("event")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--event requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?;
            // Read-only inspection needs no ownership handoff or source adoption.
            let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut result = store
                .release_status(
                    after,
                    options.flags.get("items-after").map(String::as_str),
                    event,
                )
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if result["release"].is_null() { result["release"]=serde_json::json!(inspect_current(&dir)?); }
            result["current"]=result["release"].clone();
            result["latest"]=serde_json::json!(latest(&dir).ok());
            if let Some(selector)=options.positionals.get(1) {
                let n=if selector=="latest" {latest(&dir)?} else if selector=="current" {result["release"].as_u64().ok_or("no current release")?} else {selector.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("releases selection requires N, latest or current")?};
                selectable(&dir,n)?;let root=dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string());bound_at(root.clone())?;
                result["selected"]=serde_json::json!({"release":n,"current":result["release"].as_u64()==Some(n),"digest":crate::release_files::digest(&root)?});
            }
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn prune(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "prune",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let owner = target(&dir)?;
            let held = status(&dir, owner.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let confirm = options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive");
            let result = match owner.as_ref() {
                Some(owner) => owner
                    .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
                    .and_then(|mut s| s.prune_dormant(confirm, Some(revision)))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
                None => crate::release_control::request(
                    &dir,
                    crate::release_control::Command::Prune {
                        confirm,
                        expected_revision: revision,
                    },
                )?,
            };
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());
            // Live responses are bounded. Enumerate the committed audit set (or
            // current preview) to name every item, never silently omit a drop.
            if owner.is_none() {
                let mut after = result["items_after"].as_str().map(str::to_owned);
                while let Some(key) = after {
                    let page = status(&dir, None, 0, Some(key), result["event"].as_u64())?;
                    let items = page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid inventory reply")?;
                    if items.is_empty() {
                        break;
                    }
                    println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(items).unwrap());
                    after = page["items_after"].as_str().map(str::to_owned);
                }
            }
            if !confirm {
                println!(
                    "preview only; to permanently drop this dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
                    dir.display()
                );
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn serve_release(options: &Options) -> Result<(), String> {
    let requested_port = crate::port_flag::parse("serve", options.flags.get("port").map(String::as_str))?;
    let bind = release_bind(options)?;
    let capacity = crate::host_limits::Capacity::from_options(&options.flags, |name| {
        std::env::var(name).ok()
    })?;
    let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let owner = crate::store_owner::host(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))?;
    owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
    let current = current(&dir, &owner)?.ok_or("no current release; deploy first")?;
    let release = Project {
        root: dir.join("releases").join(current.to_string())
            .canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    };
    crate::release_files::link_store(&release.root)?;
    let (port, listener) = crate::port_flag::listen("serve", requested_port, bind, None)?;
    let worker = options
        .flags
        .get("worker")
        .map(|path| {
            Path::new(path)
                .canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| format!("--worker {path}: {e}"))
        })
        .transpose()?;
    crate::serve::serve(
        release,
        port,
        crate::serve::Mode::Release {
            expected: current,
            worker,
            owner: std::sync::Arc::new(owner),
        },
        &bind.to_string(),
        listener,
        capacity,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn release_bind(options: &Options) -> Result<std::net::IpAddr, String> {
    let bind = options
        .flags
        .get("bind")
        .map(String::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("127.0.0.1");
    let address: std::net::IpAddr = bind
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| "--bind must be a literal IPv4 or IPv6 address".to_owned())?;
    if !address.is_loopback() {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("i-understand-cleartext") {
            return Err("non-loopback HTTP sends passwords and bearer tokens in cleartext; use a loopback listener behind a TLS proxy, or explicitly pass --i-understand-cleartext".into());
        }
        eprintln!(
            "WARNING: listening on {address} over CLEARTEXT HTTP. Passwords, bearer tokens, schema and data have no transport protection. Expose only through a TLS proxy on a trusted private hop; see snapback4/README.md."
        );
    }
    Ok(address)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn deployment_startup_resolves_new_paths_and_existing_aliases() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::{symlink, PermissionsExt};
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-canonical-release-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(), std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_nanos()));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).unwrap();
        struct Cleanup(std::path::PathBuf);
        impl Drop for Cleanup { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0); } }
        let _cleanup = Cleanup(root.clone());
        let options = |path: &std::path::Path| super::parse(&["deploy".into(), path.to_str().unwrap().into()]);
        let target = root.join("deployment");
        let canonical = super::canonical_directory(&options(&target)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(canonical, target.canonicalize().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&canonical).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777, 0o700);
        let alias = root.join("alias");
        symlink(&target, &alias).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&alias)).unwrap(), canonical);
        let nested = alias.join("nested");
        assert_eq!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&nested)).unwrap(), canonical.join("nested"));
        let file = root.join("file"); std::fs::write(&file, "preserved").unwrap();
        assert!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&file)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(file).unwrap(), "preserved");
    }

    #[test]
    fn release_diagnostic_fixture() {
        let Ok(outcome) = std::env::var("SB4_RELEASE_DIAGNOSTIC_FIXTURE") else {
            return;
        };
        snapback4_core::diagnostics::set_thread_start(|| {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(200))
        });
        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("release completion estimate");
        let failed = outcome == "failure";
        assert_eq!(
            super::report(
                "deploy",
                if failed {
                    Err("release fixture failure".into())
                } else {
                    Ok(())
                }
            ),
            i32::from(failed)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn release_completion_drains_a_delayed_writer_on_success_and_failure() {
        for outcome in ["success", "failure"] {
            let output = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args([
                    "--exact",
                    "verbs::releases::tests::release_diagnostic_fixture",
                    "--nocapture",
                ])
                .env("SB4_RELEASE_DIAGNOSTIC_FIXTURE", outcome)
                .output()
                .unwrap();
            assert!(output.status.success(), "{output:?}");
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
            assert!(stderr.contains("release completion estimate"), "{stderr}");
            if outcome == "failure" {
                assert!(stderr.contains("release fixture failure"), "{stderr}");
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn serving_defaults_loopback_and_requires_cleartext_acknowledgement() {
        let parse =
            |args: &[&str]| super::parse(&args.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        assert!(
            super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release"]))
                .unwrap()
                .is_loopback()
        );
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "0.0.0.0"])).is_err());
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "::"])).is_err());
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "localhost"])).is_err());
        let options = parse(&[
            "serve",
            "--i-understand-cleartext",
            "release",
            "--bind",
            "0.0.0.0",
        ]);
        assert_eq!(options.positionals, ["release"]);
        assert!(super::release_bind(&options).unwrap().is_unspecified());
    }
}

fn receipt(release:u64,generation:u64,report:&snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,options:&Options){
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"adoption":report.compact(),"details":report.summary(),"backfills":report.backfills}));}
    else {println!("release {release} is current as generation {generation}; {}",report.compact());for fill in &report.backfills {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}if options.flags.contains_key("verbose"){println!("{}",report.summary());}}
}
fn latest(dir:&Path)->Result<u64,String>{
    std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok)
        .filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|selectable(dir,*n).is_ok()))
        .max().ok_or("no complete immutable releases".into())
}
pub(super) fn check_release(options:&Options)->i32 {
    report("check",(|| {
        let store=PathBuf::from(options.flags.get("store").ok_or("--store required")?);
        let dir=store.parent().ok_or("store has no deployment directory")?;
        let selected=options.flags.get("release").ok_or("--release required")?;
        let n=if selected=="latest"{latest(dir)?}else{selected.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("--release requires N or latest")?};
        selectable(dir,n)?;
        super::release_preview::preview(&store,dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()),n,options)
    })())
}

fn selectable(dir:&Path,release:u64)->Result<(),String> {
    let root=dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let store=snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let history:bool=store.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_release_events')",[],|r|r.get(0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if history || root.join(".pending-adoption").exists() {
        let committed=if history {store.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _release_events WHERE (release=?1 OR previous=?1) AND operation!='prune')",[i64::try_from(release).map_err(|_|"release identity exceeds SQLite range")?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?} else {false};
        if !committed {return Err(format!("release {release} did not complete adoption; select a successful release with --to latest"));}
    }
    bound_at(root).map(|_|())
}

// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.

struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;

        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let mut client_compatibility=Vec::new();
    if let Some(current)=held["release"].as_u64() {
        let prior=super::releases::bound_at(store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?.join("releases").join(current.to_string()))?;
        client_compatibility=snapback4_lang::client_contracts::compare(&prior,&bound,&format!("release {current}"));
    }
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") {
        if let Some(current) = held["release"].as_u64() {
            let old_root=store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?.join("releases").join(current.to_string());
            let old=super::releases::bound_at(old_root.clone())?;
            hazards.extend(super::rollback_foresight::risks(&candidate_schema,&old.schema,current,store_path.parent().unwrap()));
            // Exact reverse adoption tests today's post-deploy image, separately from future-write risks.
            let reverse_path=snapshot.0.join("reverse.sqlite");
            engine.store().conn().execute("VACUUM INTO ?1",[reverse_path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()]).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let reverse_store=snapback4_core::Store::open(&reverse_path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let native=crate::native::prepare(&Project {root:old_root},&old).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let mut reverse=snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(current,Some(release));
            reverse.restore_dormant=true;
            reverse.native_digest=native.installed.map(|m|m.digest);
            reverse.operation="preview".into();
            if let Err(error)=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(reverse_store,old.schema,old.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&reverse,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption")) {
                hazards.push(format!("rollback to release {current} refuses on the post-deploy snapshot: {error}; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {current} --check --bridge <new-dir>` and preserve the required fields/domains in a bridge",store_path.parent().unwrap().display()));
            }
        }
    }
    hazards.extend(client_compatibility.iter().cloned());
    if options.flags.contains_key("validation-only") {
        for hazard in &hazards { eprintln!("WARNING {hazard}"); }
        return Ok(());
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"clientCompatibility":client_compatibility,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

+//! @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — operation evolution lowers into the existing shared IR.
+use crate::{ast::*, diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, type_error, unknown}};
+use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::ArgType, value::Value};
+
+fn modified(args: &[ArgDecl]) -> bool { args.iter().any(|a|a.default.is_some() || a.deprecated) }
+fn invalid(site: &crate::Site) -> Diagnostic {
+    type_error(site,"argument defaults/deprecation are supported on non-native queries and mutations only","keep ordinary parameters here; use a query/mutation for a release-compatible wrapper").guide("deploy")
+}
+pub(super) fn expand(documents: &mut [Document], diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
+    for doc in documents {
+        for p in &doc.programs {
+            if (p.native || p.kind==ProgramKind::Job) && modified(&p.args) {diagnostics.push(invalid(&p.site));}
+        }
+        for a in &doc.actions {if modified(&a.args) {diagnostics.push(invalid(&a.site));}}
+        for f in &doc.functions {if modified(&f.args) {diagnostics.push(invalid(&f.site));}}
+        let authored=doc.programs.clone();
+        for (name,target,site) in &doc.aliases {
+            let Some(p)=authored.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target && !p.native && p.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) else {
+                diagnostics.push(unknown(site,format!("alias {name} needs an authored query/mutation target {target} in this module"),"target a non-native query or mutation; aliases cannot chain").guide("deploy"));continue;
+            };
+            let mut alias=p.clone();alias.name=name.clone();alias.site=site.clone();doc.programs.push(alias);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) fn argument(arg: &ArgDecl, schema: &Schema) -> Fallible<(ArgType,Option<Value>)> {
+    let kind=crate::types::scalar_to_arg(&arg.type_);
+    let bad=||type_error(&arg.site,format!("default for {} must be a fitting non-null scalar literal and cannot combine with deprecated",arg.name),"use default <literal>, or mark the unused parameter deprecated").guide("deploy");
+    let value=if let Some(expr)=&arg.default {
+        if arg.deprecated {return Err(bad());}
+        let value=match expr {
+            Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Bool(v),..}=>Value::Bool(*v),
+            Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Str(v),..}=>match arg.type_.kind {TypeKind::Ref{..}|TypeKind::Id|TypeKind::Principal=>Value::Id(v.clone()),_=>Value::String(v.clone())},
+            Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Number(v),..}=>Value::Int(v.parse().map_err(|_|bad())?),
+            Expr::Unary {op,value,..} if op=="-" => match value.as_ref() {Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(v),..}=>Value::Int(format!("-{v}").parse().map_err(|_|bad())?),_=>return Err(bad())},
+            _=>return Err(bad()),
+        };
+        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_arg(schema,&kind,&value,&arg.name).map_err(|_|bad())?;
+        Some(value)
+    } else {None};
+    let wire=if arg.default.is_some() || arg.deprecated {
+        match kind {ArgType::Optional(_)=>kind,_=>ArgType::Optional(Box::new(kind))}
+    } else {kind};
+    Ok((wire,value))
+}

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/client_contracts.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/client_contracts.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..f0e3a2b295
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/client_contracts.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,92 @@
+//! @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — compare old callers with the candidate, separately from data adoption.
+use crate::{Bound, BoundProgram, types::Ty};
+use snapback4_core::ir::{ArgType as A, ProgramKind};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+
+/// Conservative input-domain inclusion: every value accepted by old is accepted by new.
+pub fn accepts(old: &A, new: &A) -> bool {
+    if old==new {return true;}
+    match (old,new) {
+        (A::Optional(a),A::Optional(b))=>accepts(a,b),
+        (A::Optional(_),_)=>false,
+        (a,A::Optional(b))=>accepts(a,b),
+        (A::Text{min:a,max:b,format:af},A::Text{min:c,max:d,format:bf})=>c<=a && d>=b && (bf.is_none() || af==bf),
+        (A::IntRange{..},A::Int)=>true,
+        (A::IntRange{min:a,max:b},A::IntRange{min:c,max:d})=>c<=a && d>=b,
+        (A::Enum{variants:a},A::Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),
+        (A::Bytes{max:a},A::Bytes{max:b}) | (A::Image{max:a},A::Image{max:b}) |
+        (A::Video{max:a},A::Video{max:b})=>b>=a,
+        (A::Json{max_bytes:a},A::Json{max_bytes:b})=>b>=a,
+        (A::File{max:a,types:at},A::File{max:b,types:bt})=>b>=a && snapback4_core::assets::FILE_TYPES.iter().all(|t|!snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(at,t)||snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(bt,t)),
+        (A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..},A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..})=> {
+            let parts=|t:&A|match t {A::List{of,max}=>(0,*max,of.clone()),A::ListRange{of,min,max}=>(*min,*max,of.clone()),_=>unreachable!()};
+            let (a,b,at)=parts(old);let(c,d,bt)=parts(new);
+            c<=a && d>=b && match(at,bt){(_,None)=>true,(Some(a),Some(b))=>accepts(&a,&b),_=>false}
+        }
+        _=>false,
+    }
+}
+
+fn result(ty: &Ty, bound: &Bound) -> String {
+    match ty {
+        Ty::Row(name)|Ty::WriteRow(name)|Ty::Page(name,_)=> {
+            let schema=bound.schema.public_projection();
+            if let Some(table)=schema.tables.get(name) {
+                let fields: BTreeMap<_,_>=table.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|!table.engine_field(n)).map(|(n,t)|(n,format!("{t:?};private={};constraints={:?}",table.private_field(n),table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..} if field==n)).collect::<Vec<_>>()))).collect();
+                format!("{}:{name}:{fields:?}",if matches!(ty,Ty::Page(..)){"page"}else{"row"})
+            } else {format!("{ty:?}")}
+        }
+        Ty::Optional(t)=>format!("optional {}",result(t,bound)),
+        Ty::List(t,_)|Ty::Collection(t,_)=>format!("list {}",result(t,bound)),
+        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>format!("object {:?}",fields.iter().map(|(n,t)|(n,result(t,bound))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()),
+        Ty::Text(max)=>format!("text<={max}"),
+        _=>format!("{ty:?}"),
+    }
+}
+
+fn refusals(p: &BoundProgram) -> Vec<String> {
+    fn walk(v: &serde_json::Value, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
+        match v {
+            serde_json::Value::Object(o)=> {
+                if let Some(r)=o.get("Require") {out.push(format!("{}:{}",r["code"],r["message"]));}
+                for v in o.values(){walk(v,out);}
+            }
+            serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{walk(v,out);},
+            _=>{}
+        }
+    }
+    let mut out=Vec::new();walk(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program.body).unwrap(),&mut out);out
+}
+
+/// Reports are advisory: compatibility cannot license calls or prove unchanged behavior.
+pub fn compare(old: &Bound, new: &Bound, label: &str) -> Vec<String> {
+    let mut lines=Vec::new();
+    for before in old.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) {
+        let name=&before.program.name;
+        let prefix=format!("old-client compatibility ({label}), operation {name}");
+        let Some(after)=new.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*name) else {
+            lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed operation; old calls get E_OP; keep `alias {name} = replacement` for a same-module query/mutation, or retain a wrapper"));continue;
+        };
+        if before.program.kind!=after.program.kind {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: operation kind changed; retain the old endpoint kind"));continue;}
+        for (arg,kind) in &after.program.args {
+            if !before.program.args.contains_key(arg) && !matches!(kind,A::Optional(_)) {
+                lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: clients calling without new required argument {arg} get E_INPUT; give {arg} a fitting `default <literal>` or retain a compatible wrapper"));
+            }
+        }
+        for (arg,kind) in &before.program.args {
+            match after.program.args.get(arg) {
+                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument {arg}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain `{arg}: <old type> deprecated` on a query/mutation")),
+                Some(next) if !accepts(kind,next)=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); old values may get E_INPUT; retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper")),
+                _=>{}
+            }
+        }
+        let action_result_changed=old.schema.action_specs.get(name).map(|s|&s.returns)!=new.schema.action_specs.get(name).map(|s|&s.returns);
+        if action_result_changed || result(&before.result,old)!=result(&after.result,new) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: result shape/value domain changed; preserve the old projection/domain or introduce a new operation before retiring old clients"));}
+        let old_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(before,old);
+        let new_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(after,new);
+        let changed:Vec<_>=old_domains.keys().chain(new_domains.keys()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().filter(|key|old_domains.get(*key)!=new_domains.get(*key)).cloned().collect();
+        if !changed.is_empty() {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: {}; results or refusals may change even when the TypeScript type stays number/string; preserve the old domains or verify old-client handling",changed.join(", ")));}
+        if refusals(before)!=refusals(after) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: BEHAVIOR REVIEW: refusal codes/messages changed ({:?} → {:?}); verify intended denials separately from input breaks",refusals(before),refusals(after)));}
+    }
+    lines
+}

 pub(super) fn args(current: &[(String, Ty)], old: Option<&[(String, Ty)]>, since: Option<u64>) -> String {

     format!("{{ {} }}", current.iter().map(|(name, ty)| {
         let added = old.is_some_and(|old| !old.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == name));
-        let optional = ty.is_optional() || added;

         format!("{}{}{}: {}", if added { format!("/** Since release {}. */ ", since.unwrap()) } else { String::new() }, property(name), if optional { "?" } else { "" }, ty_ts(ty))
     }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; "))
 }

+

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/result_domains.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/result_domains.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..1eef7d9652
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/result_domains.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,101 @@
+//! Conservative source-domain evidence for operation results and refusal behavior.
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — TypeScript numbers/strings must not erase consumed source-domain changes.
+use crate::{Bound,BoundProgram};
+use serde_json::Value;
+use std::collections::{BTreeMap,BTreeSet};
+
+// Inspect the complete operation: result construction may flow through writes,
+// loops, merges, helper blocks or aggregate selectors. This deliberately includes
+// guard/order dependencies rather than claiming a partial lineage analysis is exact.
+fn consumed(value:&Value,tables:&mut BTreeSet<String>,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
+    match value {
+        Value::Object(object)=> {
+            // Read, scan, merge lane, recursion and write targets all carry table.
+            if let Some(table)=object.get("table").and_then(Value::as_str) {tables.insert(table.into());}
+            if let Some(field)=object.get("Field") && let Some(name)=field["name"].as_str() {fields.insert(name.into());}
+            // Aggregate, optional-field, range and sort selectors carry field directly.
+            for key in ["field","next_field"] {if let Some(field)=object.get(key).and_then(Value::as_str) {fields.insert(field.into());}}
+            for child in object.values() {consumed(child,tables,fields);}
+        }
+        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array {consumed(child,tables,fields);},
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}
+type Definitions=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
+fn definitions(value:&Value,defs:&mut Definitions) {
+    match value {
+        Value::Object(object)=> {
+            for kind in ["Let","Const","Assign"] {
+                if let Some(binding)=object.get(kind) && let Some(name)=binding["name"].as_str() {defs.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(binding["value"].clone());}
+            }
+            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,defs);}
+        }
+        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{definitions(child,defs);},
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}
+fn payload_keys(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,seen:&mut BTreeSet<String>)->Option<BTreeSet<String>> {
+    if let Some(object)=value.get("Object").and_then(Value::as_object) {return Some(object.keys().cloned().collect());}
+    if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(Value::as_str) {
+        if !seen.insert(name.into()){return None;}
+        let mut keys=BTreeSet::new();
+        for value in defs.get(name)? {keys.extend(payload_keys(value,defs,&mut seen.clone())?);}
+        return Some(keys);
+    }
+    if let Some(shape)=value.get("Shape") {
+        let mut keys=payload_keys(&shape["base"],defs,seen)?;
+        keys.extend(shape["fields"].as_object()?.keys().cloned());return Some(keys);
+    }
+    if let Some(block)=value.get("Block") {return payload_keys(&block["value"],defs,seen);}
+    None
+}
+fn writes(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,bound:&Bound,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
+    match value {
+        Value::Object(object)=> {
+            for (kind,payload) in [("Insert","row"),("InsertIfAbsent","row"),("Upsert","row"),("Update","patch"),("WriteTarget","patch")] {
+                if let Some(write)=object.get(kind) {
+                    if let Some(keys)=payload_keys(&write[payload],defs,&mut BTreeSet::new()) {fields.extend(keys);}
+                    else {
+                        // Opaque/spread write images conservatively consume their target domain.
+                        let table=if kind=="WriteTarget" {write["target"]["table"].as_str()} else {write["table"].as_str()};
+                        if let Some(table)=table.and_then(|name|bound.schema.table(name)) {fields.extend(table.columns.keys().cloned());}
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            for child in object.values(){writes(child,defs,bound,fields);}
+        }
+        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{writes(child,defs,bound,fields);},
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}
+fn row_fields(ty:&crate::types::Ty,bound:&Bound,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
+    use crate::types::Ty;
+    match ty {
+        Ty::Row(name)|Ty::WriteRow(name)|Ty::Page(name,_)=>if let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(name) {fields.extend(table.columns.keys().cloned());},
+        Ty::RowShape(parts)|Ty::RowObject(parts)=>{fields.extend(parts.keys().cloned());for ty in parts.values(){row_fields(ty,bound,fields);}},
+        Ty::Object(parts)=>for ty in parts.values(){row_fields(ty,bound,fields);},
+        Ty::Optional(ty)|Ty::List(ty,_)|Ty::Collection(ty,_)=>row_fields(ty,bound,fields),
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}
+pub(crate) fn signature(program:&BoundProgram,bound:&Bound)->BTreeMap<String,String> {
+    let mut tables=BTreeSet::new();let mut fields=BTreeSet::new();
+    let body=serde_json::to_value(&program.program.body).unwrap();
+    consumed(&body,&mut tables,&mut fields);
+    let mut defs=Definitions::new();definitions(&body,&mut defs);writes(&body,&defs,bound,&mut fields);
+    for read in snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&program.program) {
+        if let Some(index)=bound.schema.table(&read.table).and_then(|t|t.index(&read.index)) {fields.extend(index.columns());}
+    }
+    row_fields(&program.result,bound,&mut fields);
+    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
+    for name in tables {
+        let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(&name) else {continue;};
+        for field in &fields {
+            if let Some(kind)=table.columns.get(field) {
+                let constraints:Vec<_>=table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field:name,..} if name==field)).collect();
+                out.insert(format!("{name}.{field}"),format!("{kind:?};{constraints:?}"));
+            }
+        }
+    }

 succeeded in 0ms:
  file runs as native `node --test` with default isolation, in a process group
  whose first member is a tiny Node keeper. The runner closes the keeper's stdin
  on completion, stall or abort; only the keeper calls `kill(0, SIGKILL)`.
  Runner death also closes that pipe. An unexpectedly exited keeper fails the
  file and never authorizes a runner signal. Cleanup requires two consecutive
  completed inspections (50 ms apart) with no living group member. Observed leftovers
  fail as TEST_FILE_LEAK. Failed listings and unclassifiable PID/PPID/PGID
  prefixes remain fail-closed. A ps timeout at two seconds retries once with a
  60-second bound; all other command errors refuse immediately. Listings use
  asynchronous spawn so other files' timers and keeper EOF delivery keep running.
  File watchdogs and cleanup's five-second window use per-file monotonic time.
  Expiry requires another completed snapshot: a live group after the deadline
  refuses; two consecutive empty snapshots pass even when the second is late.
  Precisely
  recognized exit-time routing anomalies
  discard the whole snapshot and retry at most twice, 50 ms apart; persistent
  anomalies refuse and never confer ownership (LLP 3000 §7, QF2).
  Metadata is validated for group members and
  their visible descendants; unrelated rows only increment diagnostics.
  Signed Darwin UIDs, variable lstart spacing and comm spaces are accepted.
  Vanished members count as exited. Processes leaving the group (setsid,
  setpgid or detached spawns) are outside cleanup custody; visible descendants
  receive metadata validation but are never signalled by the runner. Native Node's exit
  status alone decides test success; reporter counts are diagnostic only.
  The reporter observes progress as Node delivers it: synchronous work that
  does not yield may buffer events in Node's child until that work finishes.
  Such an unreported interval is subject to the same stall bound. See QF Round 7.
  The M5 MacBook is the publication host; the office M1 is calibration-only.
- Client tests and their helpers must gate Linux APIs (`/proc`, `strace`, `ss`,
  `taskset`, namespace/network tools) at the test or call, or implement a
  portable fallback. Keep portable assertions running on Darwin and give each
  skipped witness an explicit reason. The client pool runs
  `test-platform-audit.test.mjs`: a cheap static census follows static named/namespace `.mjs`
  imports and named helpers and checks scoped Linux skips/branches/early returns.
  A gate in another test does not count. This conservative common-case guard
  does not prove dynamic imports, generated scripts or arbitrary control flow;
  review those paths and run affected tests on the publication Mac.
  Listener snapshots and syscall tracing are Linux qualification capabilities,
  never dependencies of the published `snapback4/proof` browser helper.
- Before a publication dry run, warm the test artifacts **in that publication
  worktree**, while the host is free of calibration work:
  `LIBSQLITE3_FLAGS=-USQLITE_ENABLE_MEMORY_MANAGEMENT cargo test --workspace --no-run`.
  Use the dry run's toolchain, target directory, profile/features, `RUSTFLAGS`
  and Cargo profile environment. Do not add `--release` or `--profile production`:
  the suite uses the default test profile. The suite still asks Cargo for the
  complete test inventory, runs every binary and runs doctests separately.
  Warming compiles without executing tests and supplies no qualification receipt.
- One owner on the core (LLP 3000 decision 4): the engine, the store and the client contract are written by the lead session; Codex lanes take the fixture generator, the reference apps, the device witnesses and the periphery. Do not reshape `contract.ts` from a lane; propose the change in the journal.
- No ceiling is raised in slice one (LLP 2000.000 §12). A thing that does not fit changes the design or waits.
- The journal is append-only and dated; never rewrite an entry. LLP 3000 §7 carries decisions that must outlive it.
- Nothing here depends on `snapback2/` at runtime. Copy source deliberately, split under 1,500 lines on the way in, and say so in the journal.
- Named lane: `~/projects/snapback-sb4` on `sb4/*` branches. Never `git stash`; commit checkpoints.
- The conformance corpus (`corpus/`) is Snapback 2's serialized IR and value
  model; the core keeps those serializations so it binds. A case the slice
  cannot run is skipped with a named reason, never counted as a pass.
- `snapback/generated/api.ts` is built by `check` and `dev`, never committed;
  the app's typecheck needs a `check` first (the suite does one).
- Point rule probes and `exists` require a unique key. A fully pinned superset
  of a required unique key is unique too (for example an explicit role index
  containing the unique conversation/principal pair). Nullable unique key
  arguments need non-null proof. Other existence checks use bounded populations
  in code. Named equalities covering a total unique key check extra columns on
  that one row without an additional index.
- The packaged CLI uses `LIBSQLITE3_FLAGS=-USQLITE_ENABLE_MEMORY_MANAGEMENT` during both publication tests and its production build (LLP 3000 §7). A plain production Cargo build retains the dependency's ordinary SQLite policy; use the same build input when comparing with the package. The variable is unnecessary at runtime.
- When creating a stage, `scripts/publish.mjs` runs the suite and refuses on red, builds the production binary, packs `snapback4-darwin-arm64` and verifies the installed digest, then publishes both packages. Bump `version` in `packages/snapback4/package.json` (and its `optionalDependencies`), `packages/snapback4-darwin-arm64/package.json`,
  `packages/snapback4-linux-x64/package.json` and `Cargo.toml` together; a test refuses a mismatch.

  stage exists, use `node scripts/publish.mjs --resume` to validate its recorded
