#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn request(dir: &Path, command: Command) -> Result<Value, String> {
    request_observed(dir,command, |_|{})
}

/// Submission remains in flight after a lost reply, even before history shows a commit.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — source cleanup needs either no submission or a definitive completed reply.
#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn request_observed(dir: &Path, command: Command, mut pending: impl FnMut(bool)) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let store = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    let unavailable = |reason: String| {
        let owner_path = store.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| store.clone());
        let detail = if reason.contains("No such file or directory") {
            "this deployment has no release control socket".to_owned()
        } else {
            reason
        };
        format!(
            "{}; release control unavailable: {detail}; stop `snapback4 serve {}`, rerun the exact command, then `snapback4 serve {}`",
            crate::store_owner::busy(&owner_path),
            dir.display(),
            dir.display()
        )
    };
    let path = address(dir, false).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let before = socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    peer(&stream).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let after = socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    if (before.dev(), before.ino()) != (after.dev(), after.ino()) {
        return Err(unavailable(
            "release control socket changed before send".into(),
        ));
    }
    stream
        .set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(120)))
        .map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    stream
        .set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)))
        .map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&Envelope {
        identity: identity(&store)?,
        command,
    })
    .unwrap();
    if bytes.len() > INPUT {
        return Err("release control command exceeds 4096 bytes".into());
    }
    let ambiguous = |reason: io::Error| {
        format!(
            "release command result is unknown: {reason}; run `snapback4 releases {}` to inspect database authority; the server continues admitted work. Do not automatically deploy another release",
            dir.display()
        )
    };
    pending(true);
    stream.write_all(&bytes).map_err(ambiguous)?;
    stream
        .shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write)
        .map_err(ambiguous)?;
    let mut reply = Vec::new();
    stream
        .take((OUTPUT + 1) as u64)
        .read_to_end(&mut reply)
        .map_err(ambiguous)?;
    if reply.len() > OUTPUT {
        return Err("release reply too large; inspect releases before retrying".into());
    }
    let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&reply).map_err(|e| {
        format!(
            "release result is unknown ({e}); inspect `snapback4 releases {}` before retrying",
            dir.display()
        )
    })?;
    match (value.get("ok"),value.get("error").and_then(Value::as_str)) {
        (Some(value),_)=>{pending(false);Ok(value.clone())},
        (_,Some(error))=>{pending(false);Err(error.into())},
        _=>Err("release result is unknown (invalid reply); inspect releases before retrying".into()),
    }
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn request(_: &Path, _: Command) -> Result<Value, String> {
    Err("local release control requires Unix".into())
}

+type Definitions=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
+fn definitions(value:&Value,defs:&mut Definitions) {
+    match value {
+        Value::Object(object)=> {
+            for kind in ["Let","Const","Assign"] {
+                if let Some(binding)=object.get(kind) && let Some(name)=binding["name"].as_str() {defs.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(binding["value"].clone());}
+            }
+            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,defs);}
+        }
+        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{definitions(child,defs);},
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}
+fn payload_keys(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,seen:&mut BTreeSet<String>)->Option<BTreeSet<String>> {
+    if let Some(object)=value.get("Object").and_then(Value::as_object) {return Some(object.keys().cloned().collect());}
+    if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(Value::as_str) {
+        if !seen.insert(name.into()){return None;}
+        let mut keys=BTreeSet::new();
+        for value in defs.get(name)? {keys.extend(payload_keys(value,defs,&mut seen.clone())?);}
+        return Some(keys);
+    }
+    if let Some(shape)=value.get("Shape") {
+        let mut keys=payload_keys(&shape["base"],defs,seen)?;
+        keys.extend(shape["fields"].as_object()?.keys().cloned());return Some(keys);
+    }
+    if let Some(block)=value.get("Block") {return payload_keys(&block["value"],defs,seen);}
+    None
+}
+fn writes(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,bound:&Bound,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
+    match value {
+        Value::Object(object)=> {
+            for (kind,payload) in [("Insert","row"),("InsertIfAbsent","row"),("Upsert","row"),("Update","patch"),("WriteTarget","patch")] {
+                if let Some(write)=object.get(kind) {
+                    if let Some(keys)=payload_keys(&write[payload],defs,&mut BTreeSet::new()) {fields.extend(keys);}
+                    else {
+                        // Opaque/spread write images conservatively consume their target domain.
+                        let table=if kind=="WriteTarget" {write["target"]["table"].as_str()} else {write["table"].as_str()};
+                        if let Some(table)=table.and_then(|name|bound.schema.table(name)) {fields.extend(table.columns.keys().cloned());}
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            for child in object.values(){writes(child,defs,bound,fields);}
+        }
+        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{writes(child,defs,bound,fields);},
+        _=>{}
+    }
+}

//! The program IR: what the Quarry binder emits and the interpreter runs, on
//! the server and on a device alike. Copied from Snapback 2 (LLP 2000.000
//! §5.1) in its serialized form, so the conformance corpus loads unchanged;
//! the held-search descriptor, a receipt-era form, is dropped.

pub mod effects;

use crate::value::Value;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn is_zero(value: &u32) -> bool {
    *value == 0
}

fn is_false(value: &bool) -> bool {
    !*value
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Program {
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: ProgramKind,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub args: BTreeMap<String, ArgType>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub read_time_granularity_ms: Option<u64>,
    pub body: Vec<Stmt>,
    /// Binder-marked top-level returned shape loop paths and their source read sites.
    /// Empty for all other programs, preserving their serialized form.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    pub returned_shapes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// A job whose body is the app's own wasm module, run by the engine's
    /// native host one chunk per commit (LLP 3000.005). The body is empty.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub native: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProgramKind {
    Query,
    Mutation,
    #[serde(rename = "job")]
    Job,
    #[serde(rename = "action")]
    Action,
}

impl ProgramKind {
    pub fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Query => "query",
            Self::Mutation => "mutation",
            Self::Job => "job",
            Self::Action => "action",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ArgType {
    Cursor,
    Id,
    Principal,
    Ref { table: String },
    Text {
        max: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_zero")]
        min: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none", deserialize_with = "deserialize_text_format")]
        format: Option<crate::schema::TextFormat>,
    },
    Bytes { max: u32 },
    Image { max: u32 },
    Video { max: u32 },
    File {
        max: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        types: Vec<String>,
    },
    Int,
    // @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — a new tag: an older device cannot decode the program, so it never skips the bound.
    IntRange { #[serde(default = "crate::schema::int_floor", skip_serializing_if = "crate::schema::is_int_floor")] min: i64, #[serde(default = "crate::schema::int_ceiling", skip_serializing_if = "crate::schema::is_int_ceiling")] max: i64 },
    Bool,
    Time,
    Json { max_bytes: u32 },
    Optional(Box<ArgType>),
    Scalar,
    List {
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        of: Option<Box<ArgType>>,
        max: u32,
    },
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — preserve List bytes at min zero; fail closed on older readers.
    ListRange {
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        of: Option<Box<ArgType>>,
        min: u32,
        max: u32,
    },
    Enum { variants: Vec<String> },
    Decimal { scale: u8 },
    Money { currency: String, scale: u8 },
}

impl ArgType {
    pub fn bounds_valid(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Text { min, max, .. } => min <= max,
            Self::IntRange { min, max } => crate::schema::int_range_valid(*min, *max),
            Self::ListRange { of, min, max } => min <= max && of.as_ref().is_none_or(|of| of.bounds_valid()),
            Self::Optional(inner) | Self::List { of: Some(inner), .. } => inner.bounds_valid(),
            _ => true,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_optional(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Optional(_))
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Stmt {
    Const { name: String, value: Expr },
    Let { name: String, value: Expr },
    Assign { name: String, value: Expr },
    Push { target: String, value: Expr },
    If { condition: Expr, then_body: Vec<Stmt>, else_body: Vec<Stmt> },
    For { binding: String, domain: LoopDomain, body: Vec<Stmt> },
    Require {
        condition: Expr,
        code: String,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        site: Option<String>,
        /// The author's text for the refusal (`require … else CODE 'message'`); the code stays the contract.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        message: Option<String>,
    },
    /// A false predicate rolls the transaction back and re-reads `current`.
    Check { condition: Expr, current: Read },
    /// A unique clash yields absence, never the conflicting row.
    InsertIfAbsent { table: String, row: Expr, result: String },
    Insert { table: String, row: Expr },
    Update { table: String, key: Expr, patch: Expr },
    Upsert { table: String, key: Expr, row: Expr },
    /// Target lookup and the old image stay inside the engine. Only a read-filtered result is bound.
    WriteTarget { target: Read, patch: Expr, upsert: bool, condition: Option<WriteCondition>, result: Option<String> },
    Delete { table: String, key: Expr },
    /// Alternate-key delete resolves inside the engine before charging hidden expiry work.
    DeleteTarget { target: Read },
    Emit { effect: String, payload: Expr, #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] once: Option<Expr> },
    Return(Expr),
    Break,
    Continue,
}
    Divide {
        rounding: Rounding,
        /// Decimal places requested by the four-argument surface. `None`
        /// preserves the dividend's already-scaled representation.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        scale: Option<u8>,
    },
    Sort { keys: Vec<SortKey> },
    MentionTokens,
    SearchPresent,
    /// A query row assertion cannot classify a metered-out point as absent.
    RequireUncapped,
    DeterministicShare,
    Count,
    Sum,
    CountCapped,
    /// `connected[p]`: how many connections the principal holds open on the
    /// server now; `exists connected[p]` is `> 0`. A device answers 1.
    Connected,
}

impl Builtin {
    pub fn arity(&self) -> usize {
        match self {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobQuery { .. } => 1,
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobAs => 2,
            Self::ReceiptSettled { .. } | Self::Extremum { .. } | Self::ProjectFields { .. } | Self::OptionalField { .. } | Self::Calendar { .. } | Self::Civil(_) | Self::Group { .. } | Self::First | Self::Last | Self::Len | Self::Lower | Self::Trim | Self::Sha256 | Self::Sort { .. } | Self::MentionTokens | Self::SearchPresent | Self::RequireUncapped | Self::Count | Self::Sum | Self::CountCapped | Self::Connected => 1,
            Self::ExtremumOf { .. } | Self::At | Self::Concat | Self::Join | Self::Min | Self::Max | Self::Divmod { .. } | Self::Divide { .. } => 2,
            Self::Slice | Self::DeterministicShare => 3,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum Rounding {
    TowardZero,
    AwayFromZero,
    Floor,
    Ceiling,
    HalfAwayFromZero,
    HalfEven,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SortKey {
    pub field: String,
    pub order: Order,
    /// The declared private field can be omitted by row projection.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "std::ops::Not::not")]
    pub optional: bool,
}

/// A point read: one indexed key, all components bound.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Read {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    pub key: Vec<Expr>,
    pub provenance: KeyProvenance,
}

/// One index-key component of a scan prefix. Snapback 2 spelled a search
/// atom here too; Snapback 4 keeps only the scalar form.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum IndexKeyExpr {
    Scalar(Expr),
    WordPrefix(Expr),
}

impl IndexKeyExpr {
    pub fn scalar(&self) -> Option<&Expr> {
        match self {
            Self::Scalar(expr) => Some(expr),
            Self::WordPrefix(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

/// Stable presence identity: fixed storage and lossless JSON on every host.
pub type VariantId = [u32; 4];

/// A bounded range over an index prefix.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Scan {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    pub prefix: Vec<IndexKeyExpr>,
    pub order: Order,
    pub take: Bound,
    /// A program existence test consumes at most its first match below page ownership.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub existence: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub after: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub before: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub range: Option<Range>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub filter: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub opaque_cursor: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub merge_site: Option<String>,
    pub provenance: KeyProvenance,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub variant: Option<crate::ir::VariantId>,
    /// Leading equalities moved into this prefix; zero keeps strict cursor scope.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_zero")]
    pub cursor_pins: u32,
}

import type { ReleaseSnapshot } from "snapback4/contract";

// A conservative signature check; a switch can still race execution (E_OP/E_INPUT).
function operationAvailable(release: ReleaseSnapshot, name: string, kind: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): boolean {
  if (!("generation" in release)) return false;
  const object = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> => !!value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
  const canonical = (value: unknown): string => JSON.stringify(value, (_, item) => object(item)
    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(item).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) : item);
  const op = release.operations[name];
  return !!op && op.kind === kind
    && Object.entries(args).every(([name, type]) => canonical(op.args[name]) === canonical(type))
    && Object.entries(op.args).every(([name, type]) => Object.hasOwn(args, name) || (object(type) && Object.hasOwn(type, "Optional")));
}

  await delay(30); assert.deepEqual(release.getSnapshot(),{loading:true});

  await until(()=>release.getSnapshot().generation===4);
  release.close(); assert.deepEqual(release.getSnapshot(),{loading:true});
});

test('online client exposes authenticated release without mounting a query and withdraws on close', async t => {
  let queries=0;
  const client=createClient({url:'http://localhost:4400',persona:'alice',fetch:async (url,init)=>{
    if (url.endsWith('/')) { assert.equal(init.headers['x-snapback-persona'],'alice'); return Response.json(catalog(2)); }
    if (url.includes('/q/')) queries++;
    return Response.json({ok:true,seq:0,generation:2});
  }});
  t.after(()=>client.close()); const stop=client.release.subscribe(()=>{}); t.after(stop);
  await until(()=>client.release.getSnapshot().generation===2);
  assert.equal(queries,0); await client.close(); assert.deepEqual(client.release.getSnapshot(),{loading:true});
});

test('catalog auth refusals and failures are visible; legacy catalog never claims a capability', async t => {
  let response=Response.json({denied:{code:'E_AUTH',family:'auth',message:'expired'}},{status:401});
  const release=servedRelease({url:'http://catalog',current:()=>true,headers:()=>({}),fetch:async()=>response.clone()});
  t.after(()=>release.close()); const stop=release.subscribe(()=>{}); t.after(stop);
  await until(()=>release.getSnapshot().denied?.code==='E_AUTH');
  response=Response.json({generation:2,ops:[{name:'newOnly',kind:'query'}],tables:[]}); release.invalidate();
  await delay(50); assert.equal(operationAvailable(release.getSnapshot(),'newOnly','query',{}),false);
});

test('a query-change-feed refusal cannot strand the independently authenticated catalog', async t => {
  let generation=1, changed=false;
  const client=createClient({url:'http://localhost:4400',persona:'alice',fetch:async (url,init)=>{
    if (url.endsWith('/')) return Response.json({...catalog(2),generation});
    if (url.endsWith('/schema')) return Response.json({generation,schema:{tables:{},operations:{}},programs:[]});
    if (url.includes('/changes')) { changed=true; return Response.json({seq:0,generation,denied:{code:'E_RULE',family:'rule',message:'change feed unavailable'}}); }
    if (url.includes('/q/')) return Response.json({data:1,complete:true,seq:0,generation,tables:[]});
    return Response.json({ok:true,seq:0,generation});
  }});
  t.after(()=>client.close()); const stop=client.release.subscribe(()=>{}); t.after(stop);
  const watch=client.observe({kind:'query',name:'read'},{}); t.after(()=>watch.close());
  const unlisten=watch.subscribe(()=>{}); t.after(unlisten);
  await until(()=>changed && watch.getSnapshot().denied?.code==='E_RULE');
  watch.close();
  await until(()=>client.release.getSnapshot().generation===1);
  generation=2;
  await until(()=>client.release.getSnapshot().generation===2);
  assert.equal(operationAvailable(client.release.getSnapshot(),'newOnly','query',{}),true);
});

test('a generation observed before first catalog dispatch survives credential initialization', async t => {
  let generation=2;
  const release=servedRelease({url:'http://catalog',headers:()=>({authorization:'one'}),current:()=>true,fetch:async()=>Response.json({...catalog(2),generation})});
  t.after(()=>release.close());
  release.hint(new Response(null,{headers:{'x-snapback-generation':'3'}}));
  const stop=release.subscribe(()=>{});t.after(stop);
  await delay(40);assert.deepEqual(release.getSnapshot(),{loading:true});
  generation=3;await until(()=>release.getSnapshot().generation===3);
});

test('a generation observed on new credentials survives the pending old catalog reply', async t => {
  let token='one', generation=2, reply, calls=0;
  const release=servedRelease({url:'http://catalog',headers:()=>({authorization:token}),current:()=>true,fetch:async()=>{
    calls++; if(calls===1)return new Promise(resolve=>{reply=resolve;});
    return Response.json({...catalog(2),generation});
  }});
  t.after(()=>release.close());const stop=release.subscribe(()=>{});t.after(stop);
  await until(()=>!!reply);token='two';
  release.hint(new Response(null,{headers:{'x-snapback-generation':'3'}}));
  reply(Response.json(catalog(2)));await until(()=>calls>=2);
  assert.deepEqual(release.getSnapshot(),{loading:true});generation=3;
  await until(()=>release.getSnapshot().generation===3);
});


test('availability permits extra optional served inputs while preserving existing inputs and kinds', () => {
  const release={generation:2,operations:{edit:{kind:'mutation',args:{...args,flag:{Optional:'Bool'}}}},tables:{}};
  assert.equal(operationAvailable(release,'edit','mutation',args),true);
  assert.equal(operationAvailable({...release,operations:{edit:{kind:'mutation',args:{...args,flag:'Bool'}}}},'edit','mutation',args),false);
  assert.equal(operationAvailable(release,'edit','query',args),false);
  assert.equal(operationAvailable(release,'edit','mutation',{text:{Text:{max:50}}}),false);
});

//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => lock(&shared.engine)
            .store_mut()
            .release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = lock(&shared.engine)
                .store_mut()
                .prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
            lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(&engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report.compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills}),
            )
        }
    }
}

//! Column admission, maintained fields and authority/identity/delivery dependencies.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#2 — the binder and raw-schema admission share this walk.
use super::*;

pub const GROUP: &str = "$parent";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ColumnOwner {
    pub table: String,
    pub column: String,
}

impl ColumnOwner {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { format!("{}.{}", self.table, self.column) }
    // Bounded labels also keep a first contribution independent of parent state.
    pub fn target(&self) -> String { format!("$column:{}", digest(&(&self.table, &self.column))) }
}

impl Schema {
    pub(crate) fn private_owner(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&ColumnOwner> {
        // Privacy also holds before admission: a duplicate ordinary declaration
        // must never shadow a private one in a raw schema.
        self.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool { self.private_owner(name).is_none() && !self.is_recount_work(name) }

    pub fn visible_tables(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter(|(name, _)| self.visible(name))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — identity remains on Table, which image-retention compares.
    pub fn public_projection(&self) -> Self {
        let mut schema = self.clone();
        // Inspection provenance is catalog side metadata, never device input.
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.authored_indexes = Default::default(); }
        schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
        schema.receipt_dependencies.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.source_reservations.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.tables.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.maintains.retain(|m| self.visible(&m.target));
        schema
    }

    pub fn refuse_private(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.is_recount_work(name) { return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_VIEW_KEY",message:"recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()}); }
        if let Some(owner) = self.private_owner(name) {
            return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
                message: format!("{} is maintained by the engine; read the parent row or write the source rows (guide maintains)", owner.label()) });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A projected device schema deliberately has metadata without private maintains.
    /// Authoritative engines must have exactly one derivation for every owned field.
    pub fn validate_column_owners(&self) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        for (name,table) in self.visible_tables() {
            for column in table.maintained.keys() {
                if self.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|&o.table == name && &o.column == column)).count() != 1 {
                    return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name}.{column} needs exactly one engine derivation; declare the column aggregate (guide maintains)")));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Maintain {
    pub fn group_fields(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        if self.private.is_some() { vec![GROUP.into()] } else { self.by.clone() }
    }

    pub fn derivation_identity(&self) -> String { digest(&(&self.kind, &self.source, &self.filter, &self.by)) }
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { self.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| self.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label) }
}

struct Walk<'a> { schema: &'a Schema, issues: Vec<SchemaIssue> }
impl Walk<'_> {
    fn issue(&mut self, path: &str, label: &str) {
        self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: format!("maintained column {label} is depended on by {path}; use an ordinary column for authority, identity, lifetime or delivery, or a separate maintain for an aggregate (guide maintains)") });
    }
    fn target(&mut self, table: &str, path: &str) {
        if self.schema.is_recount_work(table) {self.issues.push(SchemaIssue {path:path.into(),message:"E_VIEW_KEY: recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()});}
        if let Some(owner) = self.schema.private_owner(table) { self.issue(path, &owner.label()); }
    }
    fn field(&mut self, table: &str, field: &str, path: &str) {
        if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, field) {
            self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
        }
        if self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.contains_key(field)) { self.issue(path, &format!("{table}.{field}")); }
    }
    fn term(&mut self, table: &str, term: &RuleTerm, path: &str) {
        if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) = term { self.field(table, field, path); }
    }
    fn index(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, path: &str) {
        self.target(table, path);
        if let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) && let Some(index) = definition.index(index) {
            for field in definition.maintained.keys().filter(|field| index.uses_column(field)) { self.field(table, field, path); }
            for field in index.columns() {
                if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, &field) {
                    self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
                }
            }
        }
    }
    fn rule(&mut self, table: &str, rule: &RuleExpr, path: &str) {
        match rule {
            RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny => {},
            RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { self.term(table,a,path); self.term(table,b,path); },
            RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => for item in items { self.rule(table,item,path); },
            RuleExpr::Not(item) => self.rule(table,item,path),
            RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
                self.index(target,index,path);
                for term in key { self.term(table,term,path); }
                for (field,term) in equalities { self.field(target,field,path); self.term(table,term,path); }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Owned timeline rows are written only on the timeline's scoped transition
/// path, even if their stored ownership sets are forged. Mapped application
/// tables stay ordinary parents and sources: the mirror is not a source write,
/// so it never enters a transition, and delivery copies no parent fields.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#33-f3-the-following-recipes-contract-as-a-declaration — timeline writes require their scoped transition path.
pub fn timeline_issues(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    for m in &schema.maintains {
        let Some(owner) = &m.private else { continue };
        for (role, table) in [("parent", &owner.table), ("source", &m.source)] {
            if let Some(timeline) = schema.timelines.iter().find(|t| t.owns(table)) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{}.columns.{}", owner.table, owner.column),
                    message: format!("maintained column {} has timeline-owned {role} {table} in {}; the timeline writes its own tables, so use a table it maps, such as its posts, or an ordinary table (guide maintains)", owner.label(), timeline.name) });
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — raw catalogs must refuse impossible list ranges too.
fn list_bounds_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::ListRange { of, min, max } => min <= max && list_bounds_valid(of),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } => list_bounds_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — a raw catalog cannot install a range no int satisfies.
fn int_ranges_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::IntRange { min, max } => crate::schema::int_range_valid(*min, *max),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of: inner, .. } => int_ranges_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut walk = Walk { schema, issues: Vec::new() };
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        for (field, kind) in &table.columns {
            if !list_bounds_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "list minimum must not exceed its maximum".into() });
            }
            if !int_ranges_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "an int range needs -(2^53 - 1) <= min <= max <= 2^53 - 1".into() });
            }
        }
    }
    walk.issues.extend(timeline_issues(schema));
    let mut targets = BTreeSet::new();
    for (position, m) in schema.maintains.iter().enumerate() {
        if !targets.insert(&m.target) {
            let label = schema.private_owner(&m.target).map_or_else(|| m.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label);
            walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("maintains[{position}]"),
                message: format!("duplicate maintain target {label}; each target needs exactly one derivation (guide maintains)") });
        }
    }
    for (name, table) in schema.visible_tables() {
        for (field, identity) in &table.maintained {
            if identity.is_empty() || table.defaults.contains_key(field) || table.recorded.contains_key(field)
                || !matches!(table.columns.get(field), Some(ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. })) {
                walk.issue(&format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"), &format!("{name}.{field}"));
            }
        }
        for (effect, rule) in [("read", &table.rules.read), ("insert", &table.rules.insert), ("update", &table.rules.update), ("delete", &table.rules.delete)] {
            walk.rule(name, rule, &format!("tables.{name}.rules.{effect}"));
        }
        for (index_name,index) in &table.indexes {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.indexes.{index_name}");
            if index.unique || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| m.row_key.is_some()) {
                walk.index(name,index_name,&path);
            }
            for component in &index.components {
                if let IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } = component { for field in fields { walk.field(name,field,&path); } }
                if let IndexComponent::Contribution(m) = component {
                    walk.target(&m.target,&path);
                    walk.target(&m.source,&path);
                    for field in m.dependencies() { walk.field(&m.source,&field,&path); }
        if let Some(k)=&m.row_key {for field in k.fields() {walk.field(&k.table,&field,&path);}}
                }
            }
        }
        for constraint in &table.constraints {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.constraint");
            match constraint {
                Constraint::Unique { index } => walk.index(name,index,&path),
                Constraint::Cap { by: fields, .. } | Constraint::Immutable { fields } => for field in fields { walk.field(name,field,&path); },
                Constraint::Text { field, .. } => walk.field(name,field,&path),
                Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert { table: target, index, key } => {
                    walk.index(target,index,&path);
                    for term in key { walk.term(name,term,&path); }
                },
                Constraint::AppendOnly | Constraint::Recorded | Constraint::Capabilities(_) => {},
            }
        }
        if let Some(field) = &table.expire { walk.field(name,field,&format!("tables.{name}.expire")); }
        for field in table.defaults.keys() { walk.field(name,field,&format!("tables.{name}.default")); }
        for (field,capture) in &table.recorded {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.recorded.{field}");
            walk.field(name,&capture.reference,&path);
            walk.target(&capture.table,&path);
            walk.field(&capture.table,&capture.column,&path);
        }
        if let Some(sync) = &table.sync {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.sync");
            for (audience, horizon) in sync.arms() {
                if let Some(horizon) = horizon { walk.index(name,&horizon.by,&path); }
                match audience {
                    Audience::Public => {},
                    Audience::Column(field) => walk.field(name,field,&path),
                    Audience::Target { table: target, prefix, principal } => {
                        walk.target(target,&path);
                        for field in prefix { walk.field(name,field,&path); walk.field(target,field,&path); }
                        walk.field(target,principal,&path);
                        // Resolved rule probes above carry the target index's actual columns.
                    },
                }
            }
        }
        for (field,kind) in &table.columns {
            if let ColumnType::Ref { table: target } = kind.required() { walk.target(target,&format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}")); }
        }
    }
    for m in &schema.maintains {
        let path = format!("maintain {}",m.label());
        if let Some(rule) = &m.readable { walk.rule(&m.target,rule,&path); }
        if schema.maintain_for(&m.source).is_some() {
            walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.clone(), message: format!("{path} takes generated source {}; use ordinary source rows; aggregate cascades are not supported (guide maintains)", schema.maintain_for(&m.source).unwrap().label()) });
        }
        for field in m.dependencies() { walk.field(&m.source,&field,&path); }
        if let Some(k)=&m.row_key {for field in k.fields() {walk.field(&k.table,&field,&path);}}
        if let Some(owner) = &m.private {
            if schema.maintain_for(&owner.table).is_some() {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path, message: format!("maintained column {} belongs to generated table {}; declare it on an ordinary parent table instead (guide maintains)",owner.label(),owner.table) });
                continue;
            }
            let valid = m.target == owner.target() && m.by.len() == 1 && m.computed.is_empty() && m.readable.is_none()
                && schema.table(&owner.table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.get(&owner.column) == Some(&m.derivation_identity()))
                && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_expiring()
                    && t.columns.get(&m.by[0]).is_some_and(|kind| kind.required() == &ColumnType::Ref { table: owner.table.clone() }))
                && schema.table(&m.target).is_some_and(|t| t.sync.is_none() && t.rules == Rules::deny()
                    && schema.table(&owner.table).is_some_and(|parent|parent.columns.get(&owner.column) == t.columns.get("value")));
            if !valid { walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path, message: format!("{} requires one reference from a non-expiring ordinary source and a private engine-only target; use a separate maintain for expiring sources (guide maintains)",owner.label()) }); }
        }
    }
    walk.issues
}

//! Capture bare searches, then admit optional indexes against their bound plans.
//! The authored device program never acquires a membership predicate.
use super::catalog::Catalog;
use crate::ast::*;

// @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a plain unique search order lowers to the same
// scoped words index as column order, before needle/browse variants diverge.
pub(super) fn named_order(table: &super::catalog::TableInfo, read: &mut ReadExpr) -> bool {
    if read.matching.is_none() || !read.order_keys.is_empty()
        || super::validate_key_names(read).is_err() { return false; }
    let Some(index) = table.indexes.iter().find(|index| Some(&index.name) == read.index.as_ref()) else { return false; };
    if !index.unique || !index.is_plain() || read.keys.len() > index.columns.len() { return false; }
    // Preserve the declared prefix contract, including positional arguments.
    // A named key outside that prefix must still be refused by the binder.
    if read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column| !index.columns[..read.keys.len()].contains(column))) { return false; }
    let mut pinned: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = read.keys.iter().enumerate()
        .map(|(n, key)| key.column.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| index.columns[n].clone())).collect();
    let mut predicates = Vec::new();
    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { super::intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut predicates); }
    for predicate in predicates {
        if let Some((column, _)) = super::intent::equality(&predicate, table) { pinned.insert(column); }
    }
    read.order_keys = index.columns.iter().filter(|column| !pinned.contains(*column)).cloned().collect();
    if read.order_keys.is_empty() { read.order_keys.push("id".into()); }
    for (key, column) in read.keys.iter_mut().zip(&index.columns) { key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone()); }
    // Keep the authored choice until the alternatives are planned: a compatible
    // browse branch must not become ambiguous with an equivalent plain index.
    true
}

// @ref LLP 3000.028#3.3 — only a single bare ordinary search is inferred.
pub(super) fn capture(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for program in &doc.programs {
            if program.kind != ProgramKind::Query || program.held || !program.prelude.is_empty() { continue; }
            let [Stmt::Return { value, .. }] = program.body.as_slice() else { continue };
            let value = match value { Expr::Shape { base, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base, .. } => base.as_ref(), value => value };
            let Expr::Read(read) = value else { continue };
            // Column order also identifies normalized named unique reads. An
            // explicit words index without column order still stays out.
            if read.matching.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty()
                || read.range.is_none() || read.order_keys.is_empty()
                || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|value| !scalar(value)) { continue; }
            let Some(table) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) else { continue };
            if table.maintained.is_some() { continue; }
            let Some(rule) = table.decl.rules.iter().find(|r| r.effect == Effect::Read) else { continue };
            let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = &rule.value else { continue };
            let Some(members) = catalog.tables.get(&probe.table) else { continue };
            if members.decl.expire.is_some() || members.maintained.is_some() { continue; }
            let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
            catalog.ordinary_searches.push(super::operation_name(&module, &program.name));
        }
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.028#3.3 — optional indexes must neither reject a baseline
// program nor change its IR. Admit only after the bound audience and portable
// key/label checks succeed; a declined candidate leaves no schema cost.
pub(super) fn provision(catalog: &mut Catalog, schema: &mut snapback4_core::schema::Schema, programs: &[super::BoundProgram]) {
    use snapback4_core::{ir, schema::{IndexComponent, RuleExpr, RuleTerm}, search_plan::ordinary};
    for bound in programs {
        if !catalog.ordinary_searches.contains(&bound.program.name) { continue; }
        let Some(scan) = ir::scans(&bound.program).into_iter().find(|scan|
            matches!(scan.prefix.last(), Some(ir::IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_)))) else { continue };
        let table = schema.tables[&scan.table].clone();
        let RuleExpr::Exists { table: members, index: point, key, .. } = &table.rules.read else { continue };
        let [RuleTerm::Field(scope), RuleTerm::Viewer] = key.as_slice() else { continue };
        let mut index = table.indexes[&scan.index].clone();
        // Prefix exactly the bound ordinary index. Its equality pins, word
        // position and full cursor ordering suffix must survive unchanged.
        index.components.insert(0, IndexComponent::Column(scope.clone()));
        let existing_words = table.indexes.iter().find(|(_, old)| old.components == index.components).map(|(name, _)| name.clone());
        let info = &catalog.tables[&scan.table];
        let Some(mut declaration) = info.indexes.iter().find(|i| i.name == scan.index).cloned() else { continue };
        declaration.columns.insert(0, scope.clone());
        let Some((position, _)) = &mut declaration.words else { continue };
        *position += 1;
        declaration.site = bound.site.clone();
        let new_words = existing_words.is_none();
        declaration.name = existing_words.unwrap_or_else(|| super::intent::index_name(info, &declaration));
        let mut candidate = schema.clone();
        candidate.tables.get_mut(&scan.table).unwrap().indexes.insert(declaration.name.clone(), index.clone());
        // Online-only memberships do not get sync's reverse audience index.
        // Provision it in the same speculative candidate as the target words
        // index; neither is retained when admission or portable bounds fail.
        let source_info = &catalog.tables[members];
        let source_table = &schema.tables[members];
        let columns = source_table.index(point).map(|index| index.columns()).unwrap_or_default();
        let [parent, principal] = columns.as_slice() else { continue };
        let source_columns = vec![principal.clone(), parent.clone(), "id".into()];
        let source_decl = if source_table.indexes.values().any(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns().starts_with(&source_columns[..2])) { None }
            else {
                let Some(mut source) = source_info.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.is_plain()).cloned() else { continue };
                source.columns = source_columns.clone(); source.unique = false; source.words = None;
                source.site = bound.site.clone(); source.name = super::intent::index_name(source_info, &source);
                candidate.tables.get_mut(members).unwrap().indexes.insert(source.name.clone(), snapback4_core::schema::Index {
                    components: source_columns.into_iter().map(IndexComponent::Column).collect(), unique: false,
                });
                Some(source)
            };
        if ordinary::plan(&candidate, &bound.program).is_none() { continue; }
        let mut errors = Vec::new();
        super::key_bound::check(catalog, &candidate, &mut errors);
        if !errors.is_empty() || !snapback4_core::schema::validate(&candidate).is_empty() { continue; }
        if let Some(source) = source_decl {
            schema.tables.get_mut(members).unwrap().indexes.insert(source.name.clone(), candidate.tables[members].indexes[&source.name].clone());
            catalog.synthesized.insert((members.clone(), source.name.clone()));
            catalog.tables.get_mut(members).unwrap().indexes.push(source);
        }
        if new_words {
            schema.tables.get_mut(&scan.table).unwrap().indexes.insert(declaration.name.clone(), index);
            catalog.synthesized.insert((scan.table.clone(), declaration.name.clone()));
            catalog.tables.get_mut(&scan.table).unwrap().indexes.push(declaration);
        }
    }
}

fn scalar(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    match value {
        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Name { .. } => true,
        Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => scalar(value),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => scalar(left) && scalar(right),
        _ => false,
    }

                        print::read_expr(&planned)
                    } else {

                        planned.index = Some(name.clone());

                    };
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: its nullable unique key allows tied null rows without an id tiebreak", read.table, index.name), rewrite).guide("indexes"));

                return Err(type_error(matching.site(), format!("matching takes present text, got {}", t.name()), rewrite));
            }
            prefix.push(IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(e));
        }

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
//! Quarry for Snapback 4: one language for tables, rules and programs
//! (LLP 2000.000 §5.1), bound to the core IR in Rust so `check` runs in
//! milliseconds with no other runtime.

pub mod ast;
pub mod bind;
pub mod diag;
pub mod emit;
pub mod guide;
pub mod lexer;
pub mod parser;
pub mod print;
pub mod source;
pub mod recipe;
mod repair;
pub mod types;
pub mod release_changes;
pub mod client_contracts;
mod result_domains;

pub use bind::{Bound, BoundProgram, bind};
pub use bind::security;
pub use diag::{Diagnostic, Site};
pub use parser::parse_document;

/// Parse and bind a set of `(file name, source)` documents.
pub fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)]) -> Bound {
    let mut documents = Vec::new();
    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    for (file, source) in sources {
        let (document, mut parsed) = parse_document(file, source);
        diagnostics.append(&mut parsed);
        documents.push(document);
    }
    // Refusals still block binding. On these malformed forms only, complete
    // their repairs with column types/table names from the whole project,
    // including files and declarations that follow the offending operation.
    let repair_files: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = diagnostics.iter().filter(|d|
        d.code == "E_QUARRY_SELF_EQUALITY" || (d.code == "E_QUARRY_UNSUPPORTED" && d.message.starts_with("count(*)")))
        .map(|d| d.site.file.clone()).collect();
    if !repair_files.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<_> = documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.tables.iter().cloned()).collect();
        // Use the ordinary binder's projected types, including renamed and
        // computed fields. Invalid or non-scalar projections get no invented
        // argument type. This discarded binding is diagnostic-only.
        let views = if documents.iter().any(|doc| !doc.views.is_empty() || !doc.feed_views.is_empty()) {
            let mut declarations = documents.clone();
            for doc in &mut declarations {
                doc.programs.clear(); doc.actions.clear(); doc.routes.clear(); doc.schedules.clear();
            }
            bind(declarations).view_types
        } else { Default::default() };
        diagnostics.retain(|d| !repair_files.contains(&d.site.file));
        for ((file, source), document) in sources.iter().zip(&mut documents) {
            if repair_files.contains(file) {
                let (repaired, mut parsed) = parser::parse_with_types(file, source, tables.clone(), views.clone());
                *document = repaired;
                diagnostics.append(&mut parsed);
            }
        }
    }
    bind::audience_diagnostics::parser_context(&documents, &mut diagnostics);
    repair::verify(&documents, &mut diagnostics);
    if !diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bound { invariants: Vec::new(), receipt_members: Default::default(), refusal_sites: Default::default(), view_types: Default::default(), server: documents.iter().any(|doc| doc.server), schema: Default::default(), programs: Vec::new(), personas: Vec::new(), diagnostics, notes: Vec::new() };
    }
    bind(documents)
}

//! Search card, compiled with every other keyword card by tests/guide.rs.
pub const CARD: &str = concat!(r##"# Search: word prefixes, on the server and on the device

**Start here:** Declare `search` on text or enum columns, then use `matching`, order and a cursor; the planner builds the word index. This directory query uses the profiles schema below; reset the cursor when the text changes and show incomplete results honestly.

```quarry
query people(q: text <=100, c: cursor ?):
  return profiles matching q after c first 20 by .handle
```

- `E_QUARRY_TYPE`: a search column is neither text nor enum; use one of those types before listing it in `search`.
- `N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED`: authorization varies across the scan; pin the intended room, or preserve the wider domain and show partial results.
- `E_CURSOR_INDEX`: an explicit index lacks a total order; use `by .column` so the planner supplies the page index.

## Reference

### Search one conversation, newest first, paged

Use the conversation's unique index order (or equivalently `by .ordinal`):

```quarry
table conversations:
  read <- exists members[.id, viewer]
  sync to members[.id]
table members:
  conversation: conversations
  principal: principal
  unique conversation, principal
  cap 50 by conversation
  read <- exists members[.conversation, viewer]
  sync to members[.conversation]
table messages:
  conversation: conversations
  ordinal: sequence by conversation
  body: text <=2000
  search body
  unique byConversationOrdinal: conversation, ordinal
  read <- exists members[.conversation, viewer]
  sync to members[.conversation] last 500 by byConversationOrdinal
query searchChat(conversation: conversations, q: text <=100, c: cursor ?):
  return messages[conversation = conversation] matching q last 20 by byConversationOrdinal after c
```

Both forms plan `(conversation, words(body), ordinal, id)`; the unique index still serves history and receipts.
A named plain unique index supplies its order; named bracket keys must sit inside its leading prefix. Omitting keys orders by the whole index. Other plain indexes use column order.
Existing integer ordinals work too: do not migrate to `sequence by` just to search (`guide sequences`).
Pass `next` as `c`; reset it when the conversation or text changes. An empty needle browses in ordinal order.
Keep ordinals stable while paging; refresh to include new matches before the cursor. Point decorations preserve the bare page (below).
When server and offline device searches scan the same set of messages, their ordering and examined-row,
index-entry, step and output-byte counts match. A device searches acquired history: show
`complete: false` honestly, even for an empty result beyond its horizon; follow `next` only when present.

Plain `check` counts and summarizes search warnings: unscoped completeness (`N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED`), word expansion (`N_SEARCH_WORDS`), opening refusal (`N_SEARCH_OPEN`), and per-hit roster work (`N_SEARCH_SHAPE`). `--verbose` adds explanations and rewrites; `--cost` adds execution costs.

An empty needle, including whitespace or punctuation that folds to no terms, browses in the query's order under the same read rule. This directory uses one query, ordered by handle, with `next` to continue; reset the cursor when changing the search text. For a screen that wants nothing until typing, put `if q = '': return []` before the read.

`search body, caption` declares the searchable columns. Enum columns use the same spelling, for example `search category`; their stored variant text is
tokenized exactly like text (`'coffee-shop'` contributes `coffee` and `shop`).
The planner supplies the words index: one entry per distinct word of the named text or enum columns, in that position, folded the same way everywhere
(NFC, lowercase, a word boundary at every character that is not a letter
or digit). The columns before it are the search's scope; the columns
after it order the page and end in `id`, so a cursor continues it. A
words index serves `matching` and nothing else, and cannot be unique.

Indexed tokens and query terms are both truncated to 64 Unicode characters
after folding (at most 256 UTF-8 bytes). Longer terms therefore search with
that truncated prefix. The full composite word entry, including scope,
ordering columns and appended id, must fit the portable 2048-byte index key.

`matching q` folds the text the same way. Its first term selects every
word it is a prefix of ("cof": coffee, coffers, cofounder — one seek each,
bounded by the meter for a uniform scope; at most 32 words for an admitted
non-uniform scope, whose page remains `complete: false` even below that bound), and those words
merge as ordered lanes into one page — newest first with `last 20 by .at`. Every further term must be a prefix of some word of the row ("cof
car" finds "COFFEE for Carol"). Mid-word text does not match; an expired row is gone at once;
an edit re-tokenizes. A uniform scope (the rule decided by the room)
costs one verdict per scope; with a total unique `(chatKey, principal)` key
on t, `.principal = viewer or exists t[.chatKey, viewer]` is decided by the
chat alone because the row is its own witness, so a roster page pinned by
the chat is complete and no second table is needed. An unscoped search under a per-room rule
checks the rule per row and is never `complete` (`N_NEVER_COMPLETE` names
it), except for the inferred ordinary server audience described below;
use the grouped or newest form below, or read on the device.

The newest form below merges the viewer's rooms by `at` and `id`, with

                let index = &table.indexes[&source.index];
                let pinned = index.columns().into_iter().take(source.prefix.len()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                pinned.iter().any(|name| table.columns.get(name) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))
                    && lane.scan.prefix.iter().filter_map(|key| key.scalar()).any(|key| {
                        matches!(key, Expr::Field { base, name }
                            if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding
                                || lane.setup.iter().any(|stmt| matches!(stmt, Stmt::Let { name, value: Expr::Variable(source) } if name == binding && source == &lane.binding)))
                                && !pinned.contains(name) && table.columns.get(name) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))
                    })
            });
            let matching = merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, snapback4_core::ir::IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))));
            let (message, rewrite) = if matching {
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22.4 — a grouped filtered page continues membership discovery through a meter cut.
                (format!("{} {}: the merge over {} reads {work}; a read past the {ceiling} examined ceiling returns `complete: false`", bound.program.kind.word(), bound.program.name, merge.table()),
                    "use the grouped one-cursor form in `snapback4 guide search`, section \"Search every chat I'm in\", to search every membership through a meter cut; measure with `snapback4 check --cost`")
            } else if related_principals {
                (format!("{} {}: read cost grows with the number of related principals (up to {lanes} lanes); a read past the {ceiling} examined ceiling returns `complete: false`", bound.program.kind.word(), bound.program.name),
                    "keep read-time merges for a bounded small number of related principals; for Following, declare `maintain feed: timeline` (see `snapback4 guide feeds`); measure with `snapback4 check --cost`; if a held query answers this read, delete the merge")
            } else {
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — membership search needs scope lanes; a timeline declaration addresses relationship-driven reads.
                (format!("{} {}: the merge over {} reads {work}; a read past the {ceiling} examined ceiling returns `complete: false`", bound.program.kind.word(), bound.program.name, merge.table()),
                    "measure with `snapback4 check --cost`; bound lane discovery to the scopes this read needs, and pass a cursor to continue the result; fewer lanes reduce read cost; if a held query answers this read, delete the merge")
            };
            notes.push(
                Diagnostic::new(
                    &bound.site,
                    "N_WIDE_MERGE",
                    message,
                    rewrite,
                )
                .guide(if matching { "search" } else if related_principals { "feeds" } else { "reads" }),
            );
        }
        // A held query has no server completeness class; its device still checks horizons and cuts.
        if schema.operations.get(&bound.program.name).is_some_and(|fact| fact.held) { continue; }
        // A job sweeps as the system principal, which every rule admits.
        if bound.program.kind == snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job {
            continue;
        }
        let inferred_search = snapback4_core::search_plan::ordinary::plan(schema, &bound.program);
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — grant, system and witnessed membership lanes carry their own proof.
        let proof_lanes: Vec<*const snapback4_core::ir::Scan> = snapback4_core::ir::body_merges(&bound.program.body).into_iter()
            .filter(|merge| merge.branches()).flat_map(|merge| merge.lanes.iter().filter(|lane|
                lane.grant() || lane.system() || lane.membership() || lane.guarded() || lane.exhaustive())
                .map(|lane| &lane.scan as *const _)).collect();

        let snapshot_decided = snapback4_core::engine::rules::snapshot_decided_scans(schema, &bound.program);
        for scan in snapback4_core::ir::scans(&bound.program) {
            if snapshot_decided.contains(&scan.site) { continue; }
            if proof_lanes.contains(&(scan as *const _)) { continue; }
            if inferred_search.is_some() && scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, snapback4_core::ir::IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) { continue; }
            let Some(table) = schema.table(&scan.table) else { continue };
            if table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() {
                continue;
            }
            let visibility = snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(schema, scan);
            if visibility.uniform {
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — after `require exists staff[viewer]`, every caller who reaches the page holds the grant.
            if snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_dominated(schema, &bound.program, scan) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { continue };

codex
One material concern remains at revision `225e4c2729196e36986304a08c82aaddd9659b4a`.

**P2 — Word-search source columns are missing from the consumed-domain footprint.** [result_domains.rs:87](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-rel5/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/result_domains.rs:87) gathers consulted index dependencies through `index.columns()`. That method explicitly excludes `WordPrefixes` fields ([schema.rs:714](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-rel5/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/schema.rs:714)). Consequently, an indexed search column can change domain without producing the commissioned `RESULT REVIEW` warning.

Reproduced with both versions compiling successfully:

```quarry
table items:
  label: enum('old')
  search label
  public
query q(s: text <=20):
  return items matching s first 10 by .id {id}
```

Changing `label` to `enum('old','new')` makes `client_contracts::compare(old, new, "release 1")` return `[]`. The compiled search consults a `WordPrefixes { fields: ["label"], ... }` index, but the footprint captures only `items.id`. Baseline checks therefore miss this consumed-domain change.

Gather the consulted index’s actual source fields, including `WordPrefixes.fields`, and add a regression for a search returning only IDs, with an unrelated-column control. This fits the documented conservative footprint; it requires no exact result-lineage analysis.

Validation: the existing focused `operation_evolution` test binary passed all 11 tests. I reproduced the omission using the existing language library build and confirmed its cause against fixed-revision source. No repository modifications or whole-suite runs were performed.

VERDICT: NOT READY
